## SPIRIT-OF-LAW SUMMARIES

### MINDSET
Super success requires risk, commitment, and rejecting conventional wisdom. Money follows courage, not comfort.

### DEALS
The 11-step process is iterative. Investigation never stops. Red flags are gifts.

### CAPITAL
Bankers are partners. Romance them. OPM is the only way.

### TEAM
A dream team is a requirement, not a luxury. Hire for culture and competence.

### EXECUTION
Action trumps planning. A good plan now beats a perfect plan later. 21-hour rule.

### PERCEPTION
Perception is reality in business. Image is a weapon. Structure follows strategy.

### NEGOTIATION
Structured problem-solving. Define pay-price in advance. Never share a doubt.

### STRATEGY
Consolidation is the quantum leap. Fragmented industries + professionalization = multiples.
